Run 41340843101 (commit 117c000) built image c7804efb and ran Slurm job
28161, which failed after 4:59 before serving:
- prefill-2 and decode-0 on g4 could not unpack the container: "Write on
  output file failed because No space left on device" under
  /data/enroot on g4. That is node-local disk on the compute node, not
  /scratch, and it cannot be inspected or cleaned from the login node.
  Nothing in this round's code wrote there beyond the usual container
  unpack.
- The gates ran first. The pair gate adopted, and the prepack gate
  adopted P3 on all 12 H200s under the 84-cell rule: pooled median 5.48
  us, 82 of 84 cells positive, worst cell -1.29 us, forward and reverse
  medians 5.12 and 5.71 us, outputs byte-equal to P.
- The Decode greedy gate was skipped with "insufficient remaining
  serving budget". Its condition, 1020 - SECONDS >= 90 + 802, reserved
  the whole historical P job (742 s plus 60 s) after a gate that starts
  around 160-250 s into the job, so it could never run. It now keeps
  660 s after the gate: the part of round 21's job after its prepack
  group took 634 s (41327492259), and round 17's 577 s with a fresh
  image. The tests that pinned the old expression and its skip boundary
  (now 271 s into the job instead of 129 s) follow.

This push reuses the round-22 image instead of rebuilding it, pinned as
one set: path, sha256
c7804efb3180440281a2ec1ebcff19a092786641abbcf6d70b76f9777f565bff,
12298436608 bytes and provenance, container_build_run_id 41340843101,
container_sha256, container_size_bytes, container_source_commit 117c000,
PINNED_IMAGE and the test prefixes. Everything else is as in 117c000.
If g4's node-local disk is still full, the job fails the same way within
about five minutes.
